To live authentically in a world built on illusion means first recognizing that much of what we call reality is a collective hallucination—a shadow play designed to extract our attention and labor for the benefit of a hidden few. Education, media, and culture are not neutral; they are the instruments that shape our beliefs and keep us compliant. True wealth lies not in money but in conscious awareness, in the quality of attention we bring to each moment and each creation. This requires rejecting the shallow pursuits of consumerism and status, understanding that the global economy is a rigged game favoring the powerful at the expense of the many. Geopolitical conflicts are not random but desperate attempts to maintain the perception of control by a weakening order. The hardest part is accepting that most people are deeply invested in their comfortable illusions and will resist anyone who threatens their worldview. Yet the chains that bind us are only as strong as our belief in them; they can be cast off when we choose to see clearly. True freedom lies in stepping outside the game, building meaning on your own terms, and living with the unshakable truth that reality, once perceived, can never be unseen. 
- Prof. Jiang

> If you agree with this definition, then when you say the entire system is a man-made construct, I agree with your conclusion.

> However, when you say everything can be changed, I need to add some information. Simply put, physical laws and biological laws cannot be arbitrarily changed, because they form the foundation of our world. But you will find that even such exquisite cognition of physical laws always has deviations, meaning it is also impossible for humans to directly cognize all truths from 0 to 10. For example, Newton's mechanical system is undoubtedly exquisite, but due to its deviations, it was eventually replaced by relativity, which describes reality more accurately. Relativity still has its limitations, and we still cannot consider relativity as the truth of level 10.  
>   
> So why did physical laws change, but not in the way you said they can be arbitrarily changed? Because this is the relationship between objective laws and subjective laws. Any current human cognition is a subjective description of the objective world. Therefore, if you are trying to make your cognition approach the objective world, then you cannot change it arbitrarily; you can only make your cognition, such as physics and biology, closer to the real world.  
>   
> Then there are still things that we can define arbitrarily, such as law and morality. These are things defined by humans themselves, and of course they can be changed arbitrarily as you said. For example, in the past our laws considered slavery legal, but now consider it illegal. So as long as it does not violate physical and biological laws, then theoretically it can indeed be changed arbitrarily as you said.  
>   
> If you think you are God, or that God will directly help you go from 0 to 10, then the above discussion becomes invalid. If you think there can exist a theory that clearly describes the world, just like saying a camera can capture all the real information of Paris, then the above discussion also becomes invalid and goes beyond our consensus for discussion.

I love Jiang, and I absolutely love your content. You are 100% right to illuminate the 'invisible hands' of human selfishness and the Law of Proximity—how internal 'elite overproduction' and the fight for government bailouts (between Finance and AI bubbles) are driving us toward global escalation.

However, there is a critical blind spot in the belief that a return to theocracy or 'divine soul' alignment is the solution. Jiang argues that we only find redemption through suffering and that the 'stupid' animal soul of materialism is the cause of our mess. But historically, the dogmatic 'divine' frameworks he cites are exactly what fuel the maximalist, eschatological thinking that makes these wars 'off-ramp' proof.

We see this in the decline of scientific trust in America today: when people abandon empirical reality for spiritual 'certainty,' they don’t find cohesiveness—they find justification for further destruction. If we are to reach a distant utopia—one of socialism, collective care, and group cohesiveness—it cannot be built on a 'divine soul' that welcomes the destruction of cities like Tel Aviv as a spiritual cleansing. We need a foundation built on reason and introspective scientific reality, not a swing back to the theocratic cycles that caused this mess in the first place.

Will continue to watch and support your channel, and wish you the best.

Game Theory #14: The Law of Proximity
Core Concept
The Law of Proximity states that the closer two entities are (geographical, cultural, or economic), the higher the frequency of interaction and the greater the risk of intense conflict.

Key Takeaways
• Mimetic Rivalry: Proximity causes parties to stop pursuing independent goals and focus entirely on defeating their immediate neighbors.

• Frequency of Interaction: High-density environments (cities, digital spaces) increase "game frequency," which accelerates systemic decay if not managed.

• Geopolitical Shift: Forecasts a move away from globalism toward "Fortress States" focusing on immediate physical borders and local resources.

2026 Predictions
1. Regional Ruptures: Smaller nations breaking from trade blocs to secure local water/energy.

2. Urban Fragmentation: Rise of neighborhood-level protectionism.

3. Systemic Exhaustion: Digital proximity leads to hyper-competition and social structure collapse.

Summary
The greatest threat to stability in 2026 is "proximity friction"—the conflict generated by shrinking resources between neighboring competitors.

He is an amazing general  educational resource about what is going on right now but he also makes outlandish leaps and generalizations that frankly aren't even necessary to support the overall lesson he is teaching. That should be noted, but also doesn't inherently mean he's not right. In summary, don't fall hook line and sinker for everything that comes out of his mouth AND don't throw out the baby with the bathwater. He is a smart guy, but he is not the second coming.

### **@MG-Compo**

Iran - Israël - théorie des jeux - loi de proximité
Résumé illustré : https://youtu.be/uCEBqQxx0Ww
La Source : https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nOQqGy4boBY
Comment la « loi de proximité » influence-t-elle les conflits actuels ?
Quels sont les enjeux de la rivalité entre Tel-Aviv et Jérusalem ?
Expliquez le rôle de l'intelligence humaine dans l'élimination des leaders.
Comment l'IA et la finance influencent-elles la politique américaine ?
Pourquoi le conflit entre Tel-Aviv et Jérusalem est-il si profond ?
Qu'est-ce que l'exceptionnalisme persan face à la théocratie en Iran ?
Comment la Loi de proximité explique-t-elle les assassinats de dirigeants ?
Quels sont les jeux internes qui divisent actuellement Israël ?
Comment la lutte entre finance et IA affecte-t-elle les États-Unis ?
Comment l'île de Kharg pourrait-elle devenir un point de basculement ?
Quel rôle jouerait le Pakistan si l'Arabie Saoudite entrait en guerre ?
Pourquoi l'assassinat d'Ali Larijani élimine-t-il toute option diplomatique ?
Comment l'IA et la finance influencent-elles la politique américaine ?
Quel est le rôle de la « loi de proximité » ?
Pourquoi la mort d'Ali Larijani rend-elle un cessez-le-feu impossible ?
Comment l'eschatologie chiite perçoit-elle le rôle du 12e Imam ?
Pourquoi certains rabbins voient-ils la destruction comme une opportunité spirituelle ?
Quel est le lien entre la souffrance de Job et la rédemption ?
Ce texte analyse l'escalade militaire entre l'Iran et l'Israël sous l'angle de la théorie des jeux et de la loi de proximité. L'auteur soutient que les conflits internes au sein de chaque nation, qu'ils soient politiques, religieux ou économiques, dictent les décisions stratégiques au détriment de la stabilité mondiale. Il examine comment les factions extrémistes perçoivent la destruction comme un catalyseur nécessaire à une renaissance spirituelle ou au maintien du pouvoir. En soulignant l'inefficacité des défenses face aux missiles balistiques, le récit suggère que l'élimination des leaders et le sabotage économique visent des objectifs maximalistes sans issue diplomatique. Finalement, la source prédit un effondrement de l'économie globale et une transition vers des ordres théocratiques et nationalistes radicaux.

### **@MG-Compo**
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nOQqGy4boBY&lc=UgxJKwvM6KfCzS65xnt4AaABAg)

Iran Israel game theory proximity law  -   قانون التقارب   المنطق الخفي للحرب
Illustrated summary in Egyptian Arabic / ملخص مصور باللغة العربية المصرية: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nOQqGy4boBY
The source in English/ المصدر باللغة الإنجليزية : https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nOQqGy4boBY
كيف يؤثر "قانون التقارب" على الصراعات الحالية؟
ما هي المخاطر في التنافس بين تل أبيب والقدس؟
اشرح دور الذكاء البشري في تصفية القادة.
كيف يؤثر الذكاء الاصطناعي والتمويل على السياسة الأمريكية؟
لماذا الصراع بين تل أبيب والقدس متجذرٌ إلى هذا الحد؟
ما هي الاستثناءات الفارسية في مواجهة الحكم الديني في إيران؟
كيف يفسر قانون التقارب اغتيالات القادة؟
ما هي صراعات السلطة الداخلية التي تُقسّم إسرائيل حاليًا؟
كيف يؤثر الصراع بين التمويل والذكاء الاصطناعي على الولايات المتحدة؟
كيف يمكن أن تصبح جزيرة خارك نقطة تحول؟
ما هو الدور الذي ستلعبه باكستان إذا دخلت السعودية في حرب؟
لماذا يُلغي اغتيال علي لاريجاني أي خيارات دبلوماسية؟
كيف يؤثر الذكاء الاصطناعي والتمويل على السياسة الأمريكية؟ ما هو دور "قانون التقارب"؟
لماذا يجعل مقتل علي لاريجاني وقف إطلاق النار مستحيلاً؟
كيف ينظر علم آخر الزمان الشيعي إلى دور الإمام الثاني عشر؟
 لماذا يرى بعض الحاخامات الدمار فرصة روحية؟
ما الرابط بين معاناة أيوب وخلاصه؟
يستخدم هذا التحليل نظرية الألعاب وقانون التقارب لتفسير التصعيد العسكري الشامل بين إسرائيل وإيران، مُجادلاً بأن الصراعات الداخلية للدول تُملي سلوكها الخارجي. ويؤكد المؤلف أن النخب السياسية في الولايات المتحدة وإسرائيل وإيران تستغل الحرب لخدمة مصالحها الداخلية أو رؤاها الأخروية، مُحوّلةً بذلك الصدام الجيوسياسي إلى صراع روحي ووجودي. ويسعى اغتيال القادة وتدمير البنية التحتية للطاقة إلى تحقيق أهداف قصوى تجعل أي حل دبلوماسي مستحيلاً، مما يؤدي حتماً إلى انهيار الاقتصاد العالمي. وفي نهاية المطاف، يُشير النص إلى أن هذه الفترة من المعاناة ستُجبر البشرية على التخلي عن المادية من أجل الخلاص الروحي، مُؤذنةً بالانتقال إلى نظام عالمي تُهيمن عليه الثيوقراطية والقومية.

### **@wuwu365**
[View on YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nOQqGy4boBY&lc=UgyLbzQfgDBQHamsBw54AaABAg)

一、 衝突的極限升級與雙方的「極大化目標」 影片指出，以色列與伊朗的戰爭已進入嚴重升級階段。以色列轟炸了伊朗的油田，而伊朗則以攻擊卡達（供應全球20%液化天然氣）等海灣合作委員會（GCC）的能源基礎設施，以及使用防空系統無法攔截的集束彈頭轟炸特拉維夫作為報復

。這場戰爭不能僅從傳統地緣政治角度來看，因為雙方都抱持著追求徹底毀滅的**「極大化」（maximalist）終極目標**。美國與以色列試圖徹底摧毀伊朗作為一個民族國家的能力，手段包括斬首其領導層、破壞經濟基礎，甚至企圖挑起種族衝突來將伊朗「巴爾幹化」（分裂成多個民族飛地）

。相對地，伊朗的戰略則是透過攻擊中東能源基礎設施，切斷全球對廉價能源的依賴，藉此摧毀現有的全球經濟體系，以達到重構世界現實的目的

。 隨著戰局發展，未來可能引爆幾個關鍵熱點：美國可能被迫從伊朗儲存出口石油的哈爾克島（Kharg Island）發動地面入侵，但這將導致難以脫身的「任務越界」（mission creep）

。同時，沙烏地阿拉伯若參戰，將觸發其與巴基斯坦的共同防禦條約，進而將擁有核武的巴基斯坦捲入東線戰場

。此外，伊朗的代理人（如胡塞武裝）可能會封鎖更多海上咽喉要道，這最終將迫使高度依賴中東能源的日本與南韓等國被捲入戰爭

。

二、 領導層「斬首行動」與外交死胡同 目前這場戰爭的一個異常特徵是，雙方都在高效率地暗殺對方的核心領導人。伊朗方面，具備高度影響力與談判能力的務實派領袖、戰爭實際負責人阿里·拉里賈尼（Ali Larijani）遭暗殺；同時，伊朗也成功擊殺了多名以色列高級官員，包括摩薩德（情報機構）局長，甚至鎖定了總理納坦雅胡在特拉維夫的住所

。講者用「幫派火拼」來比喻此現象：一般而言，戰爭雙方會刻意避免殺死對方的最高領導人，因為領導人是最終談判與落實和平協議的關鍵

。一旦殺死像拉里賈尼這樣的領袖，不僅會引發敵對陣營內部極端派系爭奪權力的鬥爭，更會徹底切斷停火談判的「下台階」（off-ramp），確保這場戰爭將毫無退路地打到兩敗俱傷

。講者推測，這種高精準度的暗殺無法僅靠電子訊號情報（SIGINT），必然高度依賴安插在內部的「人力情報」（HUMINT），這強烈暗示了交戰國的內部存在著嚴重的背叛與派系傾軋

。

三、 核心分析框架：「鄰近法則」（The Law of Proximity） 為了解釋各國為何會出現這種自我毀滅式的決策，講者提出了**「鄰近法則」。該法則認為：人類在生活中同時參與多場不同層次的博弈（如家庭、學校、職場、國家等），但對決策影響最大的，永遠是距離自己最「鄰近」、最切身的博弈**

。將此法則應用於國際政治，意味著國與國之間的戰爭，實際上往往是由各國「內部」為了爭奪權力的政治內戰所驅動的

。為了證明這一點，講者詳細剖析了美、以、伊三國的內部撕裂：

四、 美國的內部博弈：菁英與反菁英的生存戰 在美國，民主黨與共和黨雖然都支持這場中東戰爭，但政治算計截然不同。民主黨支持戰爭是因為他們預期戰爭會讓美國陷入泥淖並極度不受歡迎，這將一舉摧毀川普與共和黨的聲勢，確保民主黨能在未來的選舉中永久執政

。共和黨則盤算著，即使戰爭導致美國失敗，總統也能藉此啟動《緊急狀態法》來暫停或延遲選舉，並順勢推動《拯救美國法案》（要求投票出示身分證以壓制少數族裔選票），甚至在戰況不利時實施全國徵兵以取消選舉，從而鞏固極權

。 更深層次來看，美國正處於「菁英過度生產」（elite overproduction）所引發的內戰邊緣。這是一場代表現狀、全球帝國與華爾街金融業的「菁英」（主要支持民主黨），對抗代表美國優先、革命與矽谷AI科技業的「反菁英」（MAGA陣營，主要支持共和黨）之間的零和博弈

。講者指出，金融業的「私人信貸泡沫」與科技業的「AI泡沫」本質上都是相互借貸的寄生性龐氏騙局

。當中東戰爭導致外部資金斷鏈、泡沫即將破裂時，雙方都在豪賭自己能控制華府，以便讓政府資金優先為自己的產業紓困

。因此，美國的外交政策完全是這兩大利益集團爭奪國家控制權的延伸。

五、 以色列的內部博弈：世俗與神權的決裂 以色列社會的撕裂程度遠超外界想像。在10月7日遇襲前，總理納坦雅胡因嚴重的貪腐醜聞與企圖修改司法體系，正面臨百萬人抗議與牢獄之災，但這場無休止的戰爭賦予了他緊急權力，成為他保住政權與自由的唯一護身符

。 以色列更深層的衝突體現在兩大城市——「特拉維夫」與「耶路撒冷」所代表的意識形態對立上。特拉維夫代表了世俗、民主、進步、包容與國際化（講者將其比喻為追求物質與感官滿足的「動物靈魂」）；而耶路撒冷則代表了極度保守的神權政治與宗教狂熱（追求與上帝連結的「神聖靈魂」）

。對於耶路撒冷的極端猶太教派而言，特拉維夫遭到導彈摧毀並非壞事，因為這能消滅他們眼中的世俗「大撒旦」

。這些宗教狂熱份子並不關心地緣政治，他們是從**末世論（eschatological）**的角度看待這場戰爭：他們相信，正如聖經中大衛王或約伯的經歷一樣，只有透過徹底的毀滅、失去一切的苦難與折磨，以色列人才能真正懺悔、獲得救贖，並重新找回對上帝的純粹信仰，而這種極端條件正是促成「彌賽亞」降臨的先決條件

。

六、 伊朗的內部博弈：世俗民族主義與神權統治的拉扯 伊朗同樣面臨嚴重的內部撕裂，主要衝突爆發於追求科學、民主與進步的「世俗民族主義者」與掌控國家機器的「伊斯蘭神權統治者」之間

。在巨大戰爭壓力的催化下，雙方都無可避免地走向極端：神權政府正快速轉向「什葉派末世論」與殉道精神，認為這是一場迎接第十二代伊瑪目（彌賽亞）回歸以對抗美以「大撒旦」的聖戰

。這種極端化同時也迫使世俗派轉向極端的「波斯例外論」，他們認為波斯文明的偉大與延續，遠比當前伊朗伊斯蘭共和國的國家存亡更為重要

。

七、 結論與全球的未來展望 講者綜合上述分析回答了最初的問題：為何各國高層領導人會頻繁遭到精準暗殺？答案正是「鄰近法則」的作用——這些國家內部的敵對派系，為了消滅國內政敵以奪取政權，不惜暗中向敵國提供最高機密的情報，這也印證了內部博弈永遠優先於外部戰爭的殘酷現實

。 展望未來五到十年，這場衝突標誌著全球世俗金融秩序與民族主義神權秩序之間的世紀對決

。講者預測，全球趨勢將不可逆轉地走向民主衰退：伊朗將變得更加神權與極端，以色列與美國也將逐漸放棄民主制度，擁抱極權或神權模式

。此外，隨著戰爭摧毀全球化基礎，世界將迎來嚴重的經濟蕭條，人類將無法維持過去極度物質主義與個人主義的生活方式

。然而，這種由戰爭與匱乏帶來的苦難也有其深層的哲學意義：它將迫使人類放棄對物質的無盡追求（動物靈魂），轉而擁抱內省、精神信仰、家庭與社區連結（神聖靈魂），這或許正是透過苦難與悲劇來實現人類最終救贖的必經之路

。

The Xhosa cattle-killing movement of 1856–1857 was a millenarian response to colonial pressure led by the young prophetess Nongqawuse, who claimed that the ancestors had instructed the Xhosa people to slaughter their cattle and destroy their crops. She promised that once these sacrifices were complete, the ancestors would rise from the dead, drive the British colonizers into the sea, and restore a renewed, purified Xhosa nation with abundant cattle and grain. Though many Xhosa initially resisted, the movement gained momentum as the paramount chief Sarhili and other leaders eventually endorsed it, leading to the slaughter of approximately 400,000 cattle and the destruction of crops. When the prophecies failed to materialize by the promised date of February 18, 1857, the result was devastating famine that killed an estimated 40,000 Xhosa and forced many survivors to seek work in the Cape Colony, fundamentally weakening the Xhosa kingdoms and accelerating British colonial control over the region

'When all you have is a hammer, everything starts to look like a nail.'

I guess if one is a game theorist, everything looks like a game. One needs to understand that the so-called divide between Democrats and Republicans is artificial. They are both parts of the same entity, just either its right or left hands. Ditto for the other divides. One needs to apply game theory to the puppet masters who control these 'hands'. What game are they playing? These players, be they the committee of 300, the 33rd degree Masons, the Dragon society, the gang of ten of ancient Venice or the supposedly fictional High Table of the John Wick series etc, play these divides, possibly for their entertainment and profit. Unlike in the Star Trek episode 'The Gamesters of Triskelion', the pawns don't get to hear the bets that have been placed not for how many 'Quatloos'. We just get to fight it out and be manipulated and die, without understanding the bets that have been placed or whether we have succeeded for our 'Gamester'. 

It is similar to the biblical story of Job. God and the Devil have a bet. Job, completely unaware of this bet, is sorely tested. A game, so to speak, that sees his entire family destroyed, him destitute etc just to see if he would renounce God and die. 

We probably need to uncover what our puppet masters want. What bets do they place and for how much. And we need to examine how we can be unshackled. We are, indeed, like the inmates of Triskelion. 

We also need to understand that this stuff is organized like an onion. Beneath the committee of 300 etc. lies probably other smaller groups, and beneath them yet another and so on. Perhaps there is no hope for us schmucks to really get to the bottom of our dilemma. And what would we do if we suddenly found ourselves free?
